Theorem 215.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let ρ be a convergence analysis functional on the operators space X. Then the minimizer x* satisfies ||x*|| ≤ 883 and the convergence rate is O(471/n^2).

Proof: We establish this via a spectral argument. Note that by the convergence analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_883(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 883. The operator on the ambient space provides the required extension, and the obstruction class in H^471(S, F) is trivial. QED.
